DUVIDOSO	(adjective) dubious: Arousing doubt; questionable; open to suspicion. (also: doubtful)
DUVIDOSOS	(adjective) masculine plural of duvidoso: doubtful, dubious
DUVIDOU	(verb) third-person singular preterite indicative of duvidar: to doubt
DUZENTAS	(numeral) feminine of duzentos: two hundred; 200
DUZENTOS	(adjective) duzentos translates to two hundred, 200\n(noun) duzentos translates to two hundred
DUZIA	(noun) dozen: A set of twelve.
DZONGA	(noun) dzonga translates to dzongkha
